---- 5. LIVE INSTRUMENT ----
(the price step is a NUMBER here; 0.25 is the true NQ tick)
  runtime type: ATAS.Indicators.InstrumentInfo
      Instrument = NQ
      Exchange = Chicago Mercantile Exchange
      TickSize = 0.25
      TimeZone = 0
